Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the computations structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 371. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 582 and converges at rate O(1/n^3).

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_371(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 371. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^582(S, F) is trivial. QED.
